        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )

                    stats,
                )

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )

                        if fn(

                            context,

        self,
        dialect: Dialect,

        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )

                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,

                    effective_parameters,
                    context,

                )

        self,

                    )

    async def _prepare_and_execute(self, operation, parameters):
        adapt_connection = self._adapt_connection
    
        async with adapt_connection._execute_mutex:
            if not adapt_connection._started:
                await adapt_connection._start_transaction()

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()

                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )

                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )

                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:

                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):

                            break

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )

        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,

                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )

                    ),
                )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )

        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters

                    cursor,

                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )

                    ),
                )

                )

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )

    async def _prepare_and_execute(self, operation, parameters):
        adapt_connection = self._adapt_connection
    
        async with adapt_connection._execute_mutex:
            if not adapt_connection._started:
                await adapt_connection._start_transaction()
    
            if parameters is None:
                parameters = ()

                    operation, self._invalidate_schema_cache_asof

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:

                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:

                            break

        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()

                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )

            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )

            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:

                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(

                )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters

                    self,

                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)

                    ),
                )

                )

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )

    async def _prepare_and_execute(self, operation, parameters):
        adapt_connection = self._adapt_connection
    
        async with adapt_connection._execute_mutex:
            if not adapt_connection._started:
                await adapt_connection._start_transaction()
    
            if parameters is None:
                parameters = ()

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:

                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break

        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI

                    )

                        context,
                    )

        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """

                    )

                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )

        )

    async def _prepare_and_execute(self, operation, parameters):
        adapt_connection = self._adapt_connection
    
        async with adapt_connection._execute_mutex:
            if not adapt_connection._started:
                await adapt_connection._start_transaction()
    
            if parameters is None:
                parameters = ()

                prepared_stmt, attributes = await adapt_connection._prepare(
                    operation, self._invalidate_schema_cache_asof

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break

        self,

        context: ExecutionContext,

        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()

                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )

            context.parameters,
        )

            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:

                )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:

                            break

    async def _prepare_and_execute(self, operation, parameters):
        adapt_connection = self._adapt_connection
    
        async with adapt_connection._execute_mutex:
            if not adapt_connection._started:
                await adapt_connection._start_transaction()
    
            if parameters is None:
                parameters = ()

                    operation, self._invalidate_schema_cache_asof

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False

                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:

                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:

                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):

                            break

        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """

            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()

                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )

                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context
                    )

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters

                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()

                        effective_parameters,

                    ),
                )

                )

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,

                        context,

    async def _prepare_and_execute(self, operation, parameters):
        adapt_connection = self._adapt_connection
    
        async with adapt_connection._execute_mutex:
            if not adapt_connection._started:
                await adapt_connection._start_transaction()
    
            if parameters is None:
                parameters = ()
    
            try:
                prepared_stmt, attributes = await adapt_connection._prepare(
                    operation, self._invalidate_schema_cache_asof
                )

                            None,

                            None,
                            None,
                            None,
                        )
                        for attr in attributes
                    ]

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )

                        if fn(

                            context,

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break

                        str_statement,

                        context,
                    )

                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):

                            break

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )

    async def _prepare_and_execute(self, operation, parameters):
        adapt_connection = self._adapt_connection
    
        async with adapt_connection._execute_mutex:
            if not adapt_connection._started:
                await adapt_connection._start_transaction()
    
            if parameters is None:
                parameters = ()

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()

                        effective_parameters,

                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )

                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:

                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):

                            break

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,

                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:

        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()

                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )

            context.parameters,
        )

            effective_parameters = parameters[0]

        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(

                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )

                    stats,
                )

                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )

                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break

                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
